10,000 EPIC MULTILINGUAL PRIMARY STAR NAMES
Fictional names for the principal stars of planetary systems.
Inspired by Latin, Italian, German, Russian, Greek, Nordic, Japanese, Arabic and French sounds.
One-word, hyphenated, apostrophe and ceremonial stellar-title styles.
